(78)

assuming zero mean Gaussian white noise.
But since

\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-6.50554pt{}\kern 6.89442pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50554pt\kern 6.89442pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-6.50554pt{}\kern 6.89442pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50554pt\kern 6.89442pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-3.94623pt{}\kern 4.33511pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.94623pt\kern 4.33511pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-2.70763pt{}\kern 3.09651pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.70763pt\kern 3.09651pt{}}}}_{R}^{-1}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{R}^{{\kern-6.33055pt{}\kern 6.71944pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.33055pt\kern 6.71944pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{R}^{{\kern-6.33055pt{}\kern 6.71944pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.33055pt\kern 6.71944pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{R}^{{\kern-3.83734pt{}\kern 4.22623pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.83734pt\kern 4.22623pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{R}^{{\kern-2.62984pt{}\kern 3.01872pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.62984pt\kern 3.01872pt{}}}}_{c}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-6.50554pt{}\kern 6.89442pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50554pt\kern 6.89442pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-6.50554pt{}\kern 6.89442pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50554pt\kern 6.89442pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-3.94623pt{}\kern 4.33511pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.94623pt\kern 4.33511pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-2.70763pt{}\kern 3.09651pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.70763pt\kern 3.09651pt{}}}}_{R}^{-T}=\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-6.50693pt{}\kern 6.89581pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50693pt\kern 6.89581pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-6.50693pt{}\kern 6.89581pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50693pt\kern 6.89581pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-3.93262pt{}\kern 4.3215pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.93262pt\kern 4.3215pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-2.69789pt{}\kern 3.08678pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.69789pt\kern 3.08678pt{}}}}_{R}\

(79)

and by naming
\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-4.02109pt{}\kern 4.40997pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.02109pt\kern 4.40997pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-2.7611pt{}\kern 3.14998pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.7611pt\kern 3.14998pt{}}}}_{z}=\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-6.50554pt{}\kern 6.89442pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50554pt\kern 6.89442pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-6.50554pt{}\kern 6.89442pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50554pt\kern 6.89442pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-3.94623pt{}\kern 4.33511pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.94623pt\kern 4.33511pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-2.70763pt{}\kern 3.09651pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.70763pt\kern 3.09651pt{}}}}_{R}^{-1}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-4.02109pt{}\kern 4.40997pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.02109pt\kern 4.40997pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-2.7611pt{}\kern 3.14998pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.7611pt\kern 3.14998pt{}}}}
, this results in

{\rm I\kern-3.00003ptE}[\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{e}^{{\kern-3.75764pt{}\kern 4.14653pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.75764pt\kern 4.14653pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{e}^{{\kern-3.75764pt{}\kern 4.14653pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.75764pt\kern 4.14653pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{e}^{{\kern-2.1938pt{}\kern 2.58269pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.1938pt\kern 2.58269pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{e}^{{\kern-1.45589pt{}\kern 1.84477pt{}}}_{{\kern-1.45589pt\kern 1.84477pt{}}}}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{e}^{{\kern-3.75764pt{}\kern 4.14653pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.75764pt\kern 4.14653pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{e}^{{\kern-3.75764pt{}\kern 4.14653pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.75764pt\kern 4.14653pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{e}^{{\kern-2.1938pt{}\kern 2.58269pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.1938pt\kern 2.58269pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{e}^{{\kern-1.45589pt{}\kern 1.84477pt{}}}_{{\kern-1.45589pt\kern 1.84477pt{}}}}^{\mathrm{T}}]=\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-4.02109pt{}\kern 4.40997pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.02109pt\kern 4.40997pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-2.7611pt{}\kern 3.14998pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.7611pt\kern 3.14998pt{}}}}_{z}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{P}^{{\kern-5.76111pt{}\kern 6.15pt{}}}_{{\kern-5.76111pt\kern 6.15pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{P}^{{\kern-5.76111pt{}\kern 6.15pt{}}}_{{\kern-5.76111pt\kern 6.15pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{P}^{{\kern-3.46303pt{}\kern 3.85191pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.46303pt\kern 3.85191pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{P}^{{\kern-2.36249pt{}\kern 2.75137pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.36249pt\kern 2.75137pt{}}}}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-4.02109pt{}\kern 4.40997pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.02109pt\kern 4.40997pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-2.7611pt{}\kern 3.14998pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.7611pt\kern 3.14998pt{}}}}_{z}^{\mathrm{T}}+\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-6.50693pt{}\kern 6.89581pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50693pt\kern 6.89581pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-6.50693pt{}\kern 6.89581pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50693pt\kern 6.89581pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-3.93262pt{}\kern 4.3215pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.93262pt\kern 4.3215pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-2.69789pt{}\kern 3.08678pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.69789pt\kern 3.08678pt{}}}}_{R}\

(80)

That is, the transformed equation now uses a diagonal measurement noise covariance. In order to properly use the transformed, now uncorrelated measurements, the filter simply uses
\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-6.50554pt{}\kern 6.89442pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50554pt\kern 6.89442pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-6.50554pt{}\kern 6.89442pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50554pt\kern 6.89442pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-3.94623pt{}\kern 4.33511pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.94623pt\kern 4.33511pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-2.70763pt{}\kern 3.09651pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.70763pt\kern 3.09651pt{}}}}_{R}^{-1}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-4.02109pt{}\kern 4.40997pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.02109pt\kern 4.40997pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-2.7611pt{}\kern 3.14998pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.7611pt\kern 3.14998pt{}}}}
instead of
\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-4.02109pt{}\kern 4.40997pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.02109pt\kern 4.40997pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-2.7611pt{}\kern 3.14998pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.7611pt\kern 3.14998pt{}}}}
, and instead of directly computing the measurement residual with
(\mathrm{\mathbf{y}}-\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-4.02109pt{}\kern 4.40997pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.02109pt\kern 4.40997pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-2.7611pt{}\kern 3.14998pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.7611pt\kern 3.14998pt{}}}}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{x}^{{\kern-4.39027pt{}\kern 4.77916pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.39027pt\kern 4.77916pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{x}^{{\kern-4.39027pt{}\kern 4.77916pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.39027pt\kern 4.77916pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{x}^{{\kern-2.58511pt{}\kern 2.974pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.58511pt\kern 2.974pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{x}^{{\kern-1.7354pt{}\kern 2.12428pt{}}}_{{\kern-1.7354pt\kern 2.12428pt{}}}})
or
(\mathrm{\mathbf{y}}-\mathrm{\mathbf{h}}(\hat{\mathrm{\mathbf{x}}}^{-}))
, we now use
\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-6.50554pt{}\kern 6.89442pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50554pt\kern 6.89442pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-6.50554pt{}\kern 6.89442pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50554pt\kern 6.89442pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-3.94623pt{}\kern 4.33511pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.94623pt\kern 4.33511pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{U}^{{\kern-2.70763pt{}\kern 3.09651pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.70763pt\kern 3.09651pt{}}}}_{R}^{-1}(\mathrm{\mathbf{y}}-\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-6.625pt{}\kern 7.01389pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.625pt\kern 7.01389pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-4.02109pt{}\kern 4.40997pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.02109pt\kern 4.40997pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{H}^{{\kern-2.7611pt{}\kern 3.14998pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.7611pt\kern 3.14998pt{}}}}\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{x}^{{\kern-4.39027pt{}\kern 4.77916pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.39027pt\kern 4.77916pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{x}^{{\kern-4.39027pt{}\kern 4.77916pt{}}}_{{\kern-4.39027pt\kern 4.77916pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{x}^{{\kern-2.58511pt{}\kern 2.974pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.58511pt\kern 2.974pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{x}^{{\kern-1.7354pt{}\kern 2.12428pt{}}}_{{\kern-1.7354pt\kern 2.12428pt{}}}})
. Rather than using the correlated measurement noise
\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{R}^{{\kern-6.33055pt{}\kern 6.71944pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.33055pt\kern 6.71944pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{R}^{{\kern-6.33055pt{}\kern 6.71944pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.33055pt\kern 6.71944pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{R}^{{\kern-3.83734pt{}\kern 4.22623pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.83734pt\kern 4.22623pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{R}^{{\kern-2.62984pt{}\kern 3.01872pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.62984pt\kern 3.01872pt{}}}}_{c}
, the filter now will use
\mathchoice{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-6.50693pt{}\kern 6.89581pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50693pt\kern 6.89581pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-6.50693pt{}\kern 6.89581pt{}}}_{{\kern-6.50693pt\kern 6.89581pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-3.93262pt{}\kern 4.3215pt{}}}_{{\kern-3.93262pt\kern 4.3215pt{}}}}{\hphantom{}\mathbf{D}^{{\kern-2.69789pt{}\kern 3.08678pt{}}}_{{\kern-2.69789pt\kern 3.08678pt{}}}}_{R}
.
